##########################################
######   POD COMBAT TRIGGERS   ######
##########################################

###### CORE COMBAT STATE TRIGGERS ######

pod_combat_is_active_trigger = {
	has_variable = pod_combat_active
}

pod_combat_is_over_trigger = {
	# The pod_combat_is_over flag is set by pod_combat_check_group_victory_effect
	# when an entire team is wiped. All combats (including 1v1) go through the
	# same victory check, so this single flag is authoritative.
	has_variable = pod_combat_is_over
}

pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = {
	has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
	var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
}

###### AP AFFORDABILITY TRIGGER ######

pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = {
	pod_combat_current_ap_value >= $COST$
}

###### GUARD / FEINT STATE TRIGGERS ######

pod_combat_target_is_guarding_trigger = {
	# $TARGET_SIDE$ = "player" or "opponent"
	has_variable = pod_combat_$TARGET_SIDE$_is_guarding
}

pod_combat_target_is_feinted_trigger = {
	# $TARGET_SIDE$ = "player" or "opponent"
	has_variable = pod_combat_$TARGET_SIDE$_feinted
}

###### SPLAT AGGREGATE TRIGGERS ######

pod_combat_is_fera_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_trait = werewolf
		has_trait = bastet
		has_trait = mokole
	}
}

###### TEAM-ALIVE TRIGGERS ######

pod_combat_has_alive_ally_trigger = {
	# True if at least one non-PC ally slot (1-4) is populated and alive.
	OR = {
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c1_char
			var:pod_combat_c1_alive = 1
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c2_char
			var:pod_combat_c2_alive = 1
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c3_char
			var:pod_combat_c3_alive = 1
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c4_char
			var:pod_combat_c4_alive = 1
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_has_alive_enemy_trigger = {
	# True if at least one enemy slot (5-9) is populated and alive.
	OR = {
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
			var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
			var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
			var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
			var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
			var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_ai_has_available_ally_trigger = {
	# True if the current actor has at least one alive non-self
	# same-team ally available as a single-ally target.
	OR = {
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c0_char
			var:pod_combat_c0_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 0 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c1_char
			var:pod_combat_c1_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 1 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c2_char
			var:pod_combat_c2_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 2 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c3_char
			var:pod_combat_c3_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 3 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c4_char
			var:pod_combat_c4_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 4 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
			var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 5 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
			var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 6 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
			var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 7 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
			var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 8 }
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
			var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
			NOT = { var:pod_combat_current_actor = 9 }
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_ai_has_available_enemy_trigger = {
	# True if the current actor has at least one alive opposing-team
	# slot. (Opposite of pod_combat_ai_has_available_ally_trigger —
	# slots 0-4 only count when the actor is on team 1, and vice versa.)
	OR = {
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c0_char
			var:pod_combat_c0_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c1_char
			var:pod_combat_c1_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c2_char
			var:pod_combat_c2_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c3_char
			var:pod_combat_c3_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c4_char
			var:pod_combat_c4_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor >= 5
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
			var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
			var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
			var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
			var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
		}
		AND = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
			var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			var:pod_combat_current_actor <= 4
		}
	}
}

###### PER-MOVE AVAILABILITY TRIGGERS ######
# Each checks AP cost + move-specific restrictions
# All evaluate in player scope during player turn

pod_combat_can_use_strike_trigger = {
	pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = { COST = 3 }
	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target
		OR = {
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
				var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6
				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_can_use_guard_trigger = {
	pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = { COST = 2 }
	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
	NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_player_is_guarding }
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c0_soak_buff_round_tier }
			var:pod_combat_c0_soak_buff_round_tier < 3
		}
	}
}

	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c0_soak_buff_combat_tier }
			var:pod_combat_c0_soak_buff_combat_tier < 3
		}
	}
}

				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char

			}

				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }

			}
			trigger_if = {

				}
			}

					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }

pod_combat_can_use_conditioning_trigger = {
	pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = { COST = 4 }
	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target
		OR = {
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
				var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6
				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {

				OR = {

					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2

	pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = { COST = 4 }
	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }

		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target
		OR = {
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
				var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6
				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char

			}

		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}

			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }

				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {

					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {

				OR = {

			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = { COST = 4 }
	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target
		OR = {
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
				var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6
				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

		OR = {
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
				var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6
				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
		}

	pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = { COST = 3 }
	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
	POD_has_enough_gnosis_for_gift = { COUNT = 1 }
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		pod_combat_has_alive_enemy_trigger = yes
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		pod_combat_player_hp_value < pod_combat_player_max_hp_value
		pod_combat_player_agg_damage_value > 0
	}
}

pod_combat_can_use_paralyzing_stare_trigger = {
	pod_combat_can_afford_ap_trigger = { COST = 3 }
	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target
		OR = {
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
				var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6
				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }

			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}

		OR = {
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char
				var:pod_combat_c5_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6
				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char
				var:pod_combat_c6_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7
				has_variable = pod_combat_c7_char
				var:pod_combat_c7_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8
				has_variable = pod_combat_c8_char
				var:pod_combat_c8_alive = 1
			}
			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9
				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 8 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c8_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 9 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c9_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

			AND = {
				var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5
				has_variable = pod_combat_c5_char

			}
			AND = {

				has_variable = pod_combat_c6_char

				has_variable = pod_combat_c9_char
				var:pod_combat_c9_alive = 1
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pod_combat_current_turn_side
			var:pod_combat_current_turn_side = 0
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_hit_debuff_round_tier < 2
				}

			trigger_if = {

				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_hit_debuff_round_tier }

			limit = { has_variable = pod_combat_pc_target }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 5 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c5_soak_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c5_soak_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 6 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c6_soak_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c6_soak_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { var:pod_combat_pc_target = 7 }
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c7_soak_debuff_round_tier }
					var:pod_combat_c7_soak_debuff_round_tier < 1
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {

				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = pod_combat_c8_soak_debuff_round_tier }
